int barsSinceBearishWyckoff = ta.barssince(distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior = ta.barssince(bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest)
int barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior = ta.barssince(bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishWyckoffTest)
bool recentBullishWyckoff = (not na(barsSinceBullishWyckoff) and barsSinceBullishWyckoff <= contextLookback) or wyckoffContextSignal == 1
bool recentBearishWyckoff = (not na(barsSinceBearishWyckoff) and barsSinceBearishWyckoff <= contextLookback) or wyckoffContextSignal == -1
bool recentBullishAuctionTrigger = recentBullishWyckoff or (not na(barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior) and barsSinceBullishLiquidityBehavior <= contextLookback)
bool recentBearishAuctionTrigger = recentBearishWyckoff or (not na(barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior) and barsSinceBearishLiquidityBehavior <= contextLookback)

// نماذج الشموع: الشكل يقرأ سياقه السابق، ولا يصبح انعكاسًا مؤكدًا إلا بإغلاق الشمعة التالية.
float realBody = math.max(math.abs(close - open), syminfo.mintick)
float upperWick = high - math.max(open, close)
float lowerWick = math.min(open, close) - low
bool precedingDownContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or (close[1] < ema20[1] and ema20[1] <= ema50[1])
bool precedingUpContext = resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or (close[1] > ema20[1] and ema20[1] >= ema50[1])

f_candle_pattern_code() =>
    bool doji = bodyRatio <= 0.10
    int code = precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] and close >= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50 ? 1 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] and close <= open[1] and bodyRatio >= 0.50 ? 2 :
      doji and lowerWick >= candleRange * 0.60 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.10 ? 23 :
      doji and upperWick >= candleRange * 0.60 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.10 ? 24 :
      doji and upperWick >= candleRange * 0.35 and lowerWick >= candleRange * 0.35 ? 25 :
      doji ? 5 :
      precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody ? 3 :
      precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody ? 4 :
      precedingDownContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody ? 13 :
      precedingUpContext and bodyRatio <= 0.40 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody ? 14 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and open <= close[1] + syminfo.mintick and close > (open[1] + close[1]) * 0.50 and close < open[1] ? 15 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and open >= close[1] - syminfo.mintick and close < (open[1] + close[1]) * 0.50 and close > open[1] ? 16 :
      precedingDownContext[2] and close[2] < open[2] and bodyRatio[2] >= 0.55 and bodyRatio[1] <= 0.30 and math.max(open[1], close[1]) <= (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 + nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * 0.10 and close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.50 and close > (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 ? 17 :
      precedingUpContext[2] and close[2] > open[2] and bodyRatio[2] >= 0.55 and bodyRatio[1] <= 0.30 and math.min(open[1], close[1]) >= (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 - nz(safeAtr[1], safeAtr) * 0.10 and close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.50 and close < (open[2] + close[2]) * 0.50 ? 18 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and math.max(open, close) <= open[1] and math.min(open, close) >= close[1] and bodyRatio <= bodyRatio[1] * 0.75 ? 19 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and math.max(open, close) <= close[1] and math.min(open, close) >= open[1] and bodyRatio <= bodyRatio[1] * 0.75 ? 20 :
      precedingDownContext and close > open and close[1] < open[1] and math.abs(low - low[1]) <= math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 3.0) ? 21 :
      precedingUpContext and close < open and close[1] > open[1] and math.abs(high - high[1]) <= math.max(safeAtr * 0.05, syminfo.mintick * 3.0) ? 22 :
      high < high[1] and low > low[1] ? 12 :
      bodyRatio > 0.10 and bodyRatio <= 0.30 and upperWick >= realBody and lowerWick >= realBody ? 26 :
      bodyRatio <= 0.45 and lowerWick >= realBody * 2.0 and upperWick <= realBody * 1.25 ? 6 :
      bodyRatio <= 0.45 and upperWick >= realBody * 2.0 and lowerWick <= realBody * 1.25 ? 7 :
      close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.85 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.08 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.08 ? 8 :
      close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.85 and upperWick <= candleRange * 0.08 and lowerWick <= candleRange * 0.08 ? 9 :
      close > open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 27 : close < open and bodyRatio >= 0.70 ? 28 : close > open ? 10 : close < open ? 11 : 0
    code
int candlePatternCode = f_candle_pattern_code()
bool bullishReversalPatternRaw = candlePatternCode == 1 or candlePatternCode == 3 or candlePatternCode == 13 or candlePatternCode == 15 or candlePatternCode == 17 or candlePatternCode == 19 or candlePatternCode == 21 or (candlePatternCode == 23 and precedingDownContext)
bool bearishReversalPatternRaw = candlePatternCode == 2 or candlePatternCode == 4 or candlePatternCode == 14 or candlePatternCode == 16 or candlePatternCode == 18 or candlePatternCode == 20 or candlePatternCode == 22 or (candlePatternCode == 24 and precedingUpContext)
[_, _, _, _, _, sidewaysRangeHigh, sidewaysRangeLow, sidewaysRangeReady, bullishInsideBarBreakRaw, bearishInsideBarBreakRaw, bullishRangeBreakRaw, bearishRangeBreakRaw, bullishInsideBarBreakout, bearishInsideBarBreakout, bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline, bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance] = f_price_action_breakouts(usePriceActionBreakouts, sidewaysRangeBars, sidewaysRangeAtrWidth, breakBuffer, safeAtr, volumePass, volumeAvailable, relativeVolume, closeLocation, precedingDownContext, precedingUpContext, bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality)
float compressionUpperNow = convergingImportantStructure ? f_project(previousImportantHigh, previousImportantHighBar, lastImportantHigh, lastImportantHighBar, bar_index) : na
float compressionLowerNow = convergingImportantStructure ? f_project(previousImportantLow, previousImportantLowBar, lastImportantLow, lastImportantLowBar, bar_index) : na
int compressionBreakDirection = not barstate.isconfirmed or not useChartPatterns or not convergingImportantStructure or newImportantHighAnchor or newImportantLowAnchor or compressionUpperNow <= compressionLowerNow + syminfo.mintick ? 0 : close > compressionUpperNow + breakBuffer and close[1] <= compressionUpperNow[1] ? 1 : close < compressionLowerNow - breakBuffer and close[1] >= compressionLowerNow[1] ? -1 : 0
sidewaysRangeHigh := compressionBreakDirection != 0 ? compressionUpperNow : sidewaysRangeHigh
sidewaysRangeLow := compressionBreakDirection != 0 ? compressionLowerNow : sidewaysRangeLow
bullishRangeBreakRaw := bullishRangeBreakRaw or compressionBreakDirection == 1
bearishRangeBreakRaw := bearishRangeBreakRaw or compressionBreakDirection == -1
bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline := bullishRangeBreakRaw and bullishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance := bearishRangeBreakRaw and bearishBreakCandleQuality and volumePass
bool bullishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close > high[1] or bullishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bearishCandleConfirmation = useCandleConfirmation and barstate.isconfirmed and (bearishReversalPatternRaw[1] and close < low[1] or bearishInsideBarBreakout)
bool bullishImportantCandleConfirmation = bullishCandleConfirmation and (bullishWyckoffTest or bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed or bullishDemandRetest or bullishRetest or bullishChoch or bullishBos or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or accumulationSpringEvent or accumulationSpringTestEvent or accumulationSosEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or accumulationPhaseEEvent)
bool bearishImportantCandleConfirmation = bearishCandleConfirmation and (bearishWyckoffTest or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishSupplyRetest or bearishRetest or bearishChoch or bearishBos or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance or distributionUtadEvent or distributionUtadTestEvent or distributionSowEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or distributionPhaseEEvent)
int barsSinceBullishCandle = ta.barssince(bullishCandleConfirmation)
int barsSinceBearishCandle = ta.barssince(bearishCandleConfirmation)
bool recentBullishCandle = not na(barsSinceBullishCandle) and barsSinceBullishCandle <= contextLookback
bool recentBearishCandle = not na(barsSinceBearishCandle) and barsSinceBearishCandle <= contextLookback
string confirmedCandleName = f_text_code(TXT_CANDLE_TYPE, candlePatternCode[1])
var string candleState = "لا يوجد نموذج شموعي مؤكد"
if bullishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bullishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار صاعد مؤكد" : confirmedCandleName + " مؤكد"
else if bearishCandleConfirmation
    candleState := bearishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد بار هابط مؤكد" : confirmedCandleName + " مؤكد"

f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state() =>
    var float lastKeyCandleHigh = na
    var float lastKeyCandleLow = na
    var int lastKeyCandleBar = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceHigh = na
    var float highVolumeResistanceLow = na
    var int highVolumeResistanceBar = na
    var float highVolumeSupportHigh = na
    var float highVolumeSupportLow = na
    var int highVolumeSupportBar = na
    bool bullishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    bool bearishHorizontalRetestSetup = false
    string currentCandleDescription = ""
    string priceActionState = ""
    string volumeCandleLevelsState = ""
    int vsaSignal = 0
    string vsaState = ""
    // وصف آخر شمعة مغلقة: مختصر، ولا يمثل دخولًا منفردًا.
    bool strongBullCandle = candlePatternCode == 8 or candlePatternCode == 27
    bool strongBearCandle = candlePatternCode == 9 or candlePatternCode == 28
    bullishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and resistanceClusterActsAsSupport and (bullishCandleConfirmation or strongBullCandle) and vwapLongPass
    bearishHorizontalRetestSetup := barstate.isconfirmed and supportClusterActsAsResistance and (bearishCandleConfirmation or strongBearCandle) and vwapShortPass
    bool highVolumeCandle = volumeAvailable and (relativeVolume >= volumeMultiple or heatmapVolumeHighOrHigher)
    bool keyRangeCandleNow = barstate.isconfirmed and (vsaWideResult and (strongBullCandle or strongBearCandle) or highVolumeCandle and (vsaWideResult or vsaNarrowResult))
    if keyRangeCandleNow
        lastKeyCandleHigh := high
        lastKeyCandleLow := low
        lastKeyCandleBar := bar_index
    else if not na(lastKeyCandleBar) and bar_index - lastKeyCandleBar > keyCandleMaximumAge
        lastKeyCandleHigh := na
        lastKeyCandleLow := na
        lastKeyCandleBar := na

    bool highVolumePeakZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingUpContext and (nearStrongResistance or high >= nz(lastSwingHigh, high) - safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation <= 0.55 or upperWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeBottomZoneEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and highVolumeCandle and precedingDownContext and (nearStrongSupport or low <= nz(lastSwingLow, low) + safeAtr * 0.25) and (closeLocation >= 0.45 or lowerWick >= realBody)
    bool highVolumeResistanceExpired = not na(highVolumeResistanceBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeResistanceBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close > highVolumeResistanceHigh + breakBuffer)
    bool highVolumeSupportExpired = not na(highVolumeSupportBar) and (bar_index - highVolumeSupportBar > keyCandleMaximumAge or close < highVolumeSupportLow - breakBuffer)
    if highVolumeResistanceExpired
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := na
        highVolumeResistanceLow := na
        highVolumeResistanceBar := na
    if highVolumeSupportExpired
        highVolumeSupportHigh := na
        highVolumeSupportLow := na
        highVolumeSupportBar := na
    if highVolumePeakZoneEvent
        highVolumeResistanceHigh := high
        highVolumeResistanceLow := low
        highVolumeResistanceBar := bar_index
    if highVolumeBottomZoneEvent
        highVolumeSupportHigh := high
        highVolumeSupportLow := low
        highVolumeSupportBar := bar_index
    bool bullishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation >= 0.55 and (bullishSweep or bearishBreakFailed)
    bool bearishVsaAbsorption = vsaHighEffort and vsaNarrowResult and closeLocation <= 0.45 and (bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed)
    bool bullishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close > high[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == -1 or bullishSweep[1] or bearishBreakFailed[1])
    bool bearishVsaConfirmation = useVsaAnalysis and barstate.isconfirmed and vsaHighEffort[1] and vsaNarrowResult[1] and close < low[1] and (resolvedStructureBias[1] == 1 or bearishSweep[1] or bullishBreakFailed[1])
    vsaSignal := not useVsaAnalysis ? 0 : (bullishVsaConfirmation or bullishVsaAbsorption or bullishVsaEffortResult) ? 1 : (bearishVsaConfirmation or bearishVsaAbsorption or bearishVsaEffortResult) ? -1 : 0

    [lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, currentCandleDescription, priceActionState, volumeCandleLevelsState, vsaSignal, vsaState, highVolumePeakZoneEvent, highVolumeBottomZoneEvent]

[lastKeyCandleHigh, lastKeyCandleLow, lastKeyCandleBar, highVolumeResistanceHigh, highVolumeResistanceLow, highVolumeResistanceBar, highVolumeSupportHigh, highVolumeSupportLow, highVolumeSupportBar, bullishHorizontalRetestSetup, bearishHorizontalRetestSetup, currentCandleDescription, priceActionState, volumeCandleLevelsState, vsaSignal, vsaState, highVolumePeakZoneEvent, highVolumeBottomZoneEvent] = f_update_candle_volume_vsa_state()

var line highVolumeResistanceVisualLine = na
var line highVolumeSupportVisualLine = na
var line keyCandleHighVisualLine = na
var line keyCandleLowVisualLine = na
bool candleLevelVisualAllowed = marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not cleanChartMode
if barstate.islast
    highVolumeResistanceVisualLine := f_update_sr_line(highVolumeResistanceVisualLine, nz(highVolumeResistanceBar, bar_index), candleLevelVisualAllowed and not na(highVolumeResistanceHigh) and not na(highVolumeResistanceLow) ? (highVolumeResistanceHigh + highVolumeResistanceLow) * 0.50 : na, bearColor, line.style_dotted, 1, 35, false)
    highVolumeSupportVisualLine := f_update_sr_line(highVolumeSupportVisualLine, nz(highVolumeSupportBar, bar_index), candleLevelVisualAllowed and not na(highVolumeSupportHigh) and not na(highVolumeSupportLow) ? (highVolumeSupportHigh + highVolumeSupportLow) * 0.50 : na, bullColor, line.style_dotted, 1, 35, false)
    keyCandleHighVisualLine := f_update_sr_line(keyCandleHighVisualLine, nz(lastKeyCandleBar, bar_index), candleLevelVisualAllowed ? lastKeyCandleHigh : na, bearColor, line.style_dotted, 1, 48, false)
    keyCandleLowVisualLine := f_update_sr_line(keyCandleLowVisualLine, nz(lastKeyCandleBar, bar_index), candleLevelVisualAllowed ? lastKeyCandleLow : na, bullColor, line.style_dotted, 1, 48, false)

// Structural AVWAP starts from a confirmed change, liquidity event, or tested zone.
f_structural_vwap_anchor_event() =>
    barstate.isconfirmed and (bullishChoch or bearishChoch or bullishSweep or bearishSweep or bullishBreakFailed or bearishBreakFailed or bullishWyckoffTest or bearishWyckoffTest or accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent or accumulationLpsEvent or distributionLpsyEvent or bullishDemandRetest or bearishSupplyRetest or bullishTrendlineRetest or bearishTrendlineRetest or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or strongResistanceBreak or strongSupportBreak or historicalResistanceBreak or historicalSupportBreak or higherResistanceBreak or higherSupportBreak) or bullishInsideBarBreakout or bearishInsideBarBreakout or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance

bool structuralVwapAnchorEvent = f_structural_vwap_anchor_event()
f_structural_vwap_state(bool enabled, bool anchorEvent, float barVolume) =>
    var float priceVolumeState = na
    var float volumeState = na
    if not enabled
        priceVolumeState := na
        volumeState := na
    else if anchorEvent
        priceVolumeState := barVolume > 0 ? hlc3 * barVolume : na
        volumeState := barVolume > 0 ? barVolume : na
    else if not na(volumeState) and volumeState > 0 and barVolume > 0
        priceVolumeState += hlc3 * barVolume
        volumeState += barVolume
    bool ready = enabled and not na(volumeState) and volumeState > 0
    [ready, ready ? priceVolumeState / volumeState : na]

[structuralVwapReady, structuralVwapValue] = f_structural_vwap_state(useStructuralVwap and trustedVwapAvailable, structuralVwapAnchorEvent, nz(volume, 0.0))
if barstate.isconfirmed
    confirmedAnalysisVwapValue := (structuralVwapReady ? structuralVwapValue : vwapValue)
    confirmedStructuralVwapReady := structuralVwapReady
// Preserve the exact broken level before the break watcher is reset below.
float confirmedRetestLevel = bullishTrendlineRetest or bearishTrendlineRetest ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : bullishRetest or bearishRetest ? resolvedBreakEventLevel : na

f_chart_event_summary() =>
    bool bullishPatternEvent = inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed or doubleBottomConfirmed or bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline or bullishInsideBarBreakout
    bool bearishPatternEvent = headShouldersConfirmed or doubleTopConfirmed or bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance or bearishInsideBarBreakout
    bool bullishReversalEvent = bearishBreakFailed or bullishSweep or bullishHorizontalRetestSetup or qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationSpringTestEvent
    bool bearishReversalEvent = bullishBreakFailed or bearishSweep or bearishHorizontalRetestSetup or qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionUtadTestEvent
    bool bullishCandleEvent = bullishImportantCandleConfirmation
    bool bearishCandleEvent = bearishImportantCandleConfirmation
    bool bullEvent = bullishChoch or bullishPatternEvent or bullishCandleEvent or bullishReversalEvent
    bool bearEvent = bearishChoch or bearishPatternEvent or bearishCandleEvent or bearishReversalEvent
    int eventDirection = bullEvent == bearEvent ? 0 : bullEvent ? 1 : -1
    string eventText = eventDirection == 0 ? "" : bullishChoch ? "تغير اتجاه ↑" : bearishChoch ? "تغير اتجاه ↓" : inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : headShouldersConfirmed ? "رأس وكتفين" : doubleBottomConfirmed ? "قاع مزدوج" : doubleTopConfirmed ? "قمة مزدوجة" : bullishRangeBreakAfterDecline ? "اختراق نموذج" : bearishRangeBreakAfterAdvance ? "كسر نموذج" : bullishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد صاعد" : bearishInsideBarBreakout ? "انسايد هابط" : bullishCandleEvent or bearishCandleEvent ? "شمعة " + confirmedCandleName : bullishReversalEvent ? "انعكاس صاعد" : bearishReversalEvent ? "انعكاس هابط" : ""
    float eventLevel = eventDirection == 1 ? low : eventDirection == -1 ? high : close
    bool eventWarning = bullishReversalEvent or bearishReversalEvent
    bool markerEnabled = eventDirection != 0 and eventText != "" and showEventLabel
    [eventDirection, eventText, bar_index, nz(eventLevel, close), safeAtr, eventWarning ? warningColor : eventDirection == 1 ? bullColor : bearColor, markerEnabled]

[unifiedEventDirection, unifiedEventText, unifiedEventBar, unifiedEventLevel, unifiedEventAtr, unifiedEventColor, unifiedEventMarkerEnabled] = f_chart_event_summary()




// =============================================================================
// Latest trade plan only
// =============================================================================
// A technical event is snapshotted before all optional participation filters.
// A plan still requires the original qualifiers or a later confirmed follow-through.
const int SETUP_EVENT_NONE = 0
const int SETUP_EVENT_EARLY = 1
const int SETUP_EVENT_RETEST = 2
const int SETUP_EVENT_BREAK = 3
const int SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER = 0
const int SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION = 1
const int SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE = 2
const int SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN = 3
const int SETUP_SOURCE_DIVERGENCE = 4

f_setup_wave_range(bool isLong, int eventBar, int maximumAge, float swingHigh, int swingHighBar, float swingLow, int swingLowBar) =>
    bool validSequence = not na(eventBar) and not na(swingHigh) and not na(swingLow) and not na(swingHighBar) and not na(swingLowBar) and swingHighBar <= eventBar and swingLowBar <= eventBar and eventBar - math.min(swingHighBar, swingLowBar) <= maximumAge and (isLong ? swingLowBar < swingHighBar : swingHighBar < swingLowBar)
    validSequence ? math.abs(swingHigh - swingLow) : na

type SetupCandidateState
    int dir = 0
    int eventKind = 0
    int priority = 0
    bool momentumExtensionUsed = false
    int eventBar = na
    float ref = na
    float rng = na
    float stop = na
    int stopKind = 0
    bool usesGuard = false
    float guardLevel = na
    int sourceKind = 0
    string source = "-"
    bool directBreak = false
    bool needsQualifiers = false
    bool needsFvg = false
    bool qualified = false
    bool transitionEligible = false
    bool reversalEligible = false
    float tolerance = na

var SetupCandidateState longCandidateState = SetupCandidateState.new()
var SetupCandidateState shortCandidateState = SetupCandidateState.new()

f_clear_setup_candidate(SetupCandidateState candidate) =>
    candidate.dir := 0
    candidate.eventKind := SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    candidate.priority := 0
    candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := false
    candidate.eventBar := na
    candidate.ref := na
    candidate.rng := na
    candidate.stop := na
    candidate.stopKind := 0
    candidate.usesGuard := false
    candidate.guardLevel := na
    candidate.sourceKind := SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER
    candidate.source := "-"
    candidate.directBreak := false
    candidate.needsQualifiers := false
    candidate.needsFvg := false
    candidate.qualified := false
    candidate.transitionEligible := false
    candidate.reversalEligible := false
    candidate.tolerance := na
    candidate

f_set_setup_candidate(SetupCandidateState candidate, int direction, int eventKind, int priority, float reference, float measuredRange, float stopAnchor, int stopKind, bool usesGuard, float guardLevel, int sourceKind, string source, bool directBreak, bool needsQualifiers, bool needsFvg, bool qualified, bool transitionEligible, bool reversalEligible, float tolerance) =>
    candidate.dir := direction
    candidate.eventKind := eventKind
    candidate.priority := priority
    candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := false
    candidate.eventBar := bar_index
    candidate.ref := reference
    candidate.rng := measuredRange
    candidate.stop := stopAnchor
    candidate.stopKind := stopKind
    candidate.usesGuard := usesGuard
    candidate.guardLevel := guardLevel
    candidate.sourceKind := sourceKind
    candidate.source := source
    candidate.directBreak := directBreak
    candidate.needsQualifiers := needsQualifiers
    candidate.needsFvg := needsFvg
    candidate.qualified := qualified
    candidate.transitionEligible := transitionEligible
    candidate.reversalEligible := reversalEligible
    candidate.tolerance := tolerance
    candidate

type SetupInputContext
    bool firstSessionValue = false
    int chartSecondsValue = 0
    float safeAtrValue = na
    float dynamicAverageValue = na
    int movingAverageBiasValue = 0
    int structureBiasValue = 0
    int higherScoreValue = 0
    int vsaSignalValue = 0
    float closeLocationValue = na
    float bodyRatioValue = na
    int retestWindowValue = 0
    float retestToleranceValue = na
    float breakBufferValue = na
    bool earlyEngineEnabled = false
    bool requireHtfAlignment = false
    bool volumeFilterEnabled = false
    bool fvgFilterEnabled = false
    bool adaptiveEntryQuality = false
    bool preventExtended = false
    bool volumePassValue = false
    bool fvgBullPassValue = false
    bool fvgBearPassValue = false
    bool bullDirectQuality = false
    bool bearDirectQuality = false
    bool bullCandleQuality = false
    bool bearCandleQuality = false
    string entryModeValue = ""

f_update_setup_candidate_state(SetupCandidateState candidate, int directionFilter, SetupInputContext ctx) =>
    if ctx.firstSessionValue
        f_clear_setup_candidate(candidate)
    int maximumBars = ctx.chartSecondsValue <= 5 * 60 ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 16) : ctx.chartSecondsValue <= 15 * 60 ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 12) : ctx.chartSecondsValue <= 60 * 60 ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 8) : math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, 6)
    int storedCandidateMaximumBars = candidate.directBreak ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, maximumBars) : maximumBars
    bool bullStructure = bullishBreak
    bool bearStructure = bearishBreak
    bool bullChannel = majorBreakoutUpEvent
    bool bearChannel = majorBreakoutDownEvent
    bool bullTrend = bullishTrendlineBreakEvent
    bool bearTrend = bearishTrendlineBreakEvent
    int bullHorizontalBreakKind = strongResistanceBreak ? 3 : historicalResistanceBreak ? 2 : higherResistanceBreak ? 1 : 0
    int bearHorizontalBreakKind = strongSupportBreak ? 3 : historicalSupportBreak ? 2 : higherSupportBreak ? 1 : 0
    bool bullHorizontal = bullHorizontalBreakKind != 0
    bool bearHorizontal = bearHorizontalBreakKind != 0
    float bullHorizontalBreakLevel = bullHorizontalBreakKind == 3 ? confirmedResistanceBreakLevel : bullHorizontalBreakKind == 2 ? priorHistoricalResistance : bullHorizontalBreakKind == 1 ? priorHigherTimeframeResistance : na
    float bearHorizontalBreakLevel = bearHorizontalBreakKind == 3 ? confirmedSupportBreakLevel : bearHorizontalBreakKind == 2 ? priorHistoricalSupport : bearHorizontalBreakKind == 1 ? priorHigherTimeframeSupport : na
    bool bullPattern = inverseHeadShouldersConfirmed
    bool bearPattern = headShouldersConfirmed
    bool bullBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and (bullStructure or bullChannel or bullTrend or bullHorizontal or bullPattern or accumulationSosEvent or bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bullishRangeBreakRaw)
    bool bearBreak = barstate.isconfirmed and (bearStructure or bearChannel or bearTrend or bearHorizontal or bearPattern or distributionSowEvent or bearishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw)
    // بنية تنفيذية سريعة: كسر شمعة سابقة أو استرداد EMA20 من الجهة المقابلة، لا مجرد البقاء فوقه/تحته.
    bool bullMicroShift = close > open and ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.60 and (close > nz(high[1], high) or (close > ctx.dynamicAverageValue and nz(close[1], close) <= nz((ctx[1]).dynamicAverageValue, ctx.dynamicAverageValue)))
    bool bearMicroShift = close < open and ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.40 and (close < nz(low[1], low) or (close < ctx.dynamicAverageValue and nz(close[1], close) >= nz((ctx[1]).dynamicAverageValue, ctx.dynamicAverageValue)))
    float earlyLocationPenetration = math.max(ctx.retestToleranceValue, ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.75)
    bool bullDemandLocation = demandZoneActive and low <= demandZoneHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high >= demandZoneLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low >= demandZoneLow - earlyLocationPenetration and close >= demandZoneLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool bullBreakerLocation = bullishBreakerActive and low <= bullishBreakerHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high >= bullishBreakerLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low >= bullishBreakerLow - earlyLocationPenetration and close >= bullishBreakerLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool bullPivotLocation = not na(lastSwingLow) and low <= lastSwingLow + ctx.retestToleranceValue and low >= lastSwingLow - earlyLocationPenetration and close >= lastSwingLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool bearSupplyLocation = supplyZoneActive and high >= supplyZoneLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low <= supplyZoneHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high <= supplyZoneHigh + earlyLocationPenetration and close <= supplyZoneHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool bearBreakerLocation = bearishBreakerActive and high >= bearishBreakerLow - ctx.retestToleranceValue and low <= bearishBreakerHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue and high <= bearishBreakerHigh + earlyLocationPenetration and close <= bearishBreakerHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool bearPivotLocation = not na(lastSwingHigh) and high >= lastSwingHigh - ctx.retestToleranceValue and high <= lastSwingHigh + earlyLocationPenetration and close <= lastSwingHigh + ctx.retestToleranceValue
    bool bullEarlyLocation = nearStrongSupport or resistanceClusterActsAsSupport or bullPivotLocation or (not touchingStrongSupportCluster and not touchingStrongResistanceCluster and (bullDemandLocation or bullBreakerLocation))
    bool bearEarlyLocation = nearStrongResistance or supportClusterActsAsResistance or bearPivotLocation or (not touchingStrongSupportCluster and not touchingStrongResistanceCluster and (bearSupplyLocation or bearBreakerLocation))
    bool bullEarlyReversal = ctx.earlyEngineEnabled and bullMicroShift and bullEarlyLocation and (bullishSweep or bullishImportantCandleConfirmation or bullishRsiDivergence or highVolumeBottomZoneEvent) and ctx.vsaSignalValue != -1
    bool bearEarlyReversal = ctx.earlyEngineEnabled and bearMicroShift and bearEarlyLocation and (bearishSweep or bearishImportantCandleConfirmation or bearishRsiDivergence or highVolumePeakZoneEvent) and ctx.vsaSignalValue != 1
    float continuationTolerance = math.max(ctx.retestToleranceValue, ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.20)
    float bullPrimaryRetest = f_nearest_price_level(false, close, 0.0, resistanceClusterActsAsSupport ? strongResistanceHigh : na, strongSupportReady and not supportClusterActsAsResistance ? strongSupportHigh : na, lastSwingLow, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float bearPrimaryRetest = f_nearest_price_level(true, close, 0.0, supportClusterActsAsResistance ? strongSupportLow : na, strongResistanceReady and not resistanceClusterActsAsSupport ? strongResistanceLow : na, lastSwingHigh, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float bullContinuationReference = not na(bullPrimaryRetest) and close - bullPrimaryRetest <= ctx.safeAtrValue ? bullPrimaryRetest : f_nearest_price_level(false, close, 0.0, ctx.dynamicAverageValue, demandZoneActive ? demandZoneHigh : na, bullishBreakerActive ? bullishBreakerHigh : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    float bearContinuationReference = not na(bearPrimaryRetest) and bearPrimaryRetest - close <= ctx.safeAtrValue ? bearPrimaryRetest : f_nearest_price_level(true, close, 0.0, ctx.dynamicAverageValue, supplyZoneActive ? supplyZoneLow : na, bearishBreakerActive ? bearishBreakerLow : na, na, na, na, na, na, na, na)
    bool bullContinuationTrendContext = ctx.structureBiasValue == 1 or (ctx.structureBiasValue == 0 and ctx.movingAverageBiasValue == 1 and (not ctx.requireHtfAlignment or ctx.higherScoreValue >= 0))
    bool bearContinuationTrendContext = ctx.structureBiasValue == -1 or (ctx.structureBiasValue == 0 and ctx.movingAverageBiasValue == -1 and (not ctx.requireHtfAlignment or ctx.higherScoreValue <= 0))
    bool bullContinuationRetest = not na(bullContinuationReference) and bullContinuationTrendContext and low <= bullContinuationReference + continuationTolerance and low >= bullContinuationReference - ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.75 and (na(lastSwingLow) or low > lastSwingLow - ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.15) and bullMicroShift and close > bullContinuationReference and ctx.vsaSignalValue != -1
    bool bearContinuationRetest = not na(bearContinuationReference) and bearContinuationTrendContext and high >= bearContinuationReference - continuationTolerance and high <= bearContinuationReference + ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.75 and (na(lastSwingHigh) or high < lastSwingHigh + ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.15) and bearMicroShift and close < bearContinuationReference and ctx.vsaSignalValue != 1
    int bullRetestSubKind = bullishHorizontalRetestSetup ? 4 : bullishRetest ? 7 : qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout or bearishBreakFailed ? 8 : bullishTrendlineRetest ? 2 : bullishBreakerRetest ? 3 : bullishDemandRetest ? 5 : accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or accumulationLpsEvent ? 6 : bullContinuationRetest ? 1 : 0
    bool bullRetestSetup = bullRetestSubKind != 0
    int bearRetestSubKind = bearishHorizontalRetestSetup ? 4 : bearishRetest ? 7 : qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout or bullishBreakFailed ? 8 : bearishTrendlineRetest ? 2 : bearishBreakerRetest ? 3 : bearishSupplyRetest ? 5 : distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or distributionLpsyEvent ? 6 : bearContinuationRetest ? 1 : 0
    bool bearRetestSetup = bearRetestSubKind != 0
    // الانعكاس المبكر متاح في الوضع التلقائي فقط؛ أوضاع الكسر والاختبار صارمة.
    int bullEventKind = ctx.entryModeValue != ENTRY_BREAK and bullRetestSetup ? SETUP_EVENT_RETEST : ctx.entryModeValue != ENTRY_RETEST and bullBreak ? SETUP_EVENT_BREAK : ctx.entryModeValue == ENTRY_AUTO and bullEarlyReversal ? SETUP_EVENT_EARLY : SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    int bearEventKind = ctx.entryModeValue != ENTRY_BREAK and bearRetestSetup ? SETUP_EVENT_RETEST : ctx.entryModeValue != ENTRY_RETEST and bearBreak ? SETUP_EVENT_BREAK : ctx.entryModeValue == ENTRY_AUTO and bearEarlyReversal ? SETUP_EVENT_EARLY : SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    bool bullEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and bullEventKind != SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    bool bearEvent = barstate.isconfirmed and bearEventKind != SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    // أولوية حصرية لنوع الحدث؛ لا نجمع نقاط early/retest/break المتداخلة.
    int bullEventPriority = (bullEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST ? 300 : bullEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK ? 200 : bullEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY ? 100 : 0) + ((bullHorizontalBreakKind == 3 or bullRetestSubKind == 4) ? 50 : bullHorizontalBreakKind > 0 ? 45 : bullChannel ? 40 : bullTrend ? 30 : (bullStructure or bullRetestSubKind == 7) ? 25 : 0) + (ctx.bullDirectQuality ? 10 : 0) + int(math.round(ctx.closeLocationValue * 10.0))
    int bearEventPriority = (bearEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST ? 300 : bearEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK ? 200 : bearEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY ? 100 : 0) + ((bearHorizontalBreakKind == 3 or bearRetestSubKind == 4) ? 50 : bearHorizontalBreakKind > 0 ? 45 : bearChannel ? 40 : bearTrend ? 30 : (bearStructure or bearRetestSubKind == 7) ? 25 : 0) + (ctx.bearDirectQuality ? 10 : 0) + int(math.round((1.0 - ctx.closeLocationValue) * 10.0))
    int eventDirection = directionFilter == 1 and bullEvent ? 1 : directionFilter == -1 and bearEvent ? -1 : 0
    int selectedEventKind = eventDirection == 1 ? bullEventKind : eventDirection == -1 ? bearEventKind : SETUP_EVENT_NONE
    int selectedEventSubKind = selectedEventKind == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST ? (eventDirection == 1 ? bullRetestSubKind : bearRetestSubKind) : 0
    int selectedEventPriority = eventDirection == 1 ? bullEventPriority : eventDirection == -1 ? bearEventPriority : 0

    float activeRetestTolerance = nz(candidate.tolerance, ctx.retestToleranceValue)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and (candidate.dir != 0 and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar > storedCandidateMaximumBars or candidate.dir == 1 and not na(candidate.stop) and close < candidate.stop - ctx.breakBufferValue or candidate.dir == -1 and not na(candidate.stop) and close > candidate.stop + ctx.breakBufferValue or candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and (candidate.dir == 1 ? close < candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 : close > candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5))
        f_clear_setup_candidate(candidate)

    bool storedRetest = candidate.directBreak and not na(candidate.ref) and (candidate.dir == 1 ? low <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 and close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance and close > open and ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.55 and ctx.vsaSignalValue != -1 : candidate.dir == -1 ? high >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 and close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance and close < open and ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.45 and ctx.vsaSignalValue != 1 : false)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and storedRetest
        candidate.directBreak := false
        candidate.eventKind := SETUP_EVENT_RETEST
        candidate.priority := math.max(candidate.priority, 300)
        candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := false
        candidate.needsQualifiers := false
        candidate.needsFvg := false
        candidate.qualified := true
        candidate.transitionEligible := true
        // A later ordinary retest is a transition, not proof of a structural reversal.
        candidate.eventBar := bar_index
        candidate.stop := candidate.dir == 1 ? math.min(nz(candidate.stop, low), low) : math.max(nz(candidate.stop, high), high)
        candidate.stopKind := 27
        candidate.source := candidate.source + " | إعادة اختبار لاحقة"
        candidate.usesGuard := not na(candidate.ref)
        candidate.guardLevel := candidate.ref

    if barstate.isconfirmed and analysisSessionPass and eventDirection != 0
        bool isLong = eventDirection == 1
        int eventKindNow = selectedEventKind
        bool earlyNow = eventKindNow == SETUP_EVENT_EARLY
        bool retestNow = eventKindNow == SETUP_EVENT_RETEST
        bool directNow = eventKindNow == SETUP_EVENT_BREAK
        bool needsParticipation = directNow and ctx.volumeFilterEnabled
        bool needsFvg = directNow and ctx.fvgFilterEnabled
        bool qualifiedNow = earlyNow or retestNow or directNow and (isLong ? ctx.bullDirectQuality : ctx.bearDirectQuality) and (not needsParticipation or ctx.volumePassValue) and (not needsFvg or (isLong ? ctx.fvgBullPassValue : ctx.fvgBearPassValue))
        // Full counter-trend override is reserved for a confirmed structural reversal.
        bool primarySrLongPass = horizontalSrSignal != -1
        bool primarySrShortPass = horizontalSrSignal != 1
        bool majorLevelReversalNow = directNow and (isLong ? bullHorizontal and not na(bullHorizontalBreakLevel) and close > bullHorizontalBreakLevel and ctx.bullDirectQuality : bearHorizontal and not na(bearHorizontalBreakLevel) and close < bearHorizontalBreakLevel and ctx.bearDirectQuality) and (not ctx.volumeFilterEnabled or ctx.volumePassValue)
        bool reversalNow = isLong ? primarySrLongPass and (bullishChoch or accumulationSpringTestEvent or bullishWyckoffTest or (bearishBreakFailed and ctx.structureBiasValue == 1) or majorLevelReversalNow) : primarySrShortPass and (bearishChoch or distributionUtadTestEvent or bearishWyckoffTest or (bullishBreakFailed and ctx.structureBiasValue == -1) or majorLevelReversalNow)
        bool transitionNow = earlyNow or directNow or retestNow
        float genericSwingRange = f_setup_wave_range(isLong, bar_index, math.max(maximumBars * 4, pivotBars * 12), lastSwingHigh, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLow, lastSwingLowBar)
        float retestReference = isLong ? selectedEventSubKind == 1 ? bullContinuationReference : selectedEventSubKind == 2 ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? bullishBreakerHigh : selectedEventSubKind == 4 ? strongResistanceLevel : selectedEventSubKind == 5 ? demandZoneHigh : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? (accumulationSpringTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : bullishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : wyckoffRangeResistance) : selectedEventSubKind == 7 ? confirmedRetestLevel : selectedEventSubKind == 8 ? (qualifiedLowerChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : resolvedBreakEventLevel) : na : selectedEventSubKind == 1 ? bearContinuationReference : selectedEventSubKind == 2 ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? bearishBreakerLow : selectedEventSubKind == 4 ? strongSupportLevel : selectedEventSubKind == 5 ? supplyZoneLow : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? (distributionUtadTestEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : bearishWyckoffTest ? wyckoffTestLevel : wyckoffRangeSupport) : selectedEventSubKind == 7 ? confirmedRetestLevel : selectedEventSubKind == 8 ? (qualifiedUpperChannelFakeout ? effectiveChannelFakeoutLevel : resolvedBreakEventLevel) : na
        float reference = isLong ? earlyNow ? close : retestNow ? retestReference : bullHorizontal ? bullHorizontalBreakLevel : bullTrend ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : bullStructure ? lastSwingHigh : bullPattern ? ihsNecklineNow : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? high[2] : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bullChannel ? channelUpperNow : accumulationSosEvent ? wyckoffRangeResistance : nz(lastSwingHigh, close) : earlyNow ? close : retestNow ? retestReference : bearHorizontal ? bearHorizontalBreakLevel : bearTrend ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : bearStructure ? lastSwingLow : bearPattern ? hsNecklineNow : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? low[2] : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeLow : bearChannel ? channelLowerNow : distributionSowEvent ? wyckoffRangeSupport : nz(lastSwingLow, close)
        float retestMeasuredRange = selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? math.abs(isLong ? bullishBreakerHigh - bullishBreakerLow : bearishBreakerHigh - bearishBreakerLow) : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? math.abs(nz(wyckoffRangeResistance, close) - nz(wyckoffRangeSupport, close)) : genericSwingRange
        float measuredRange = retestNow ? retestMeasuredRange : isLong and bullPattern ? inverseHeadShouldersHeight : not isLong and bearPattern ? headShouldersHeight : (isLong ? bullishInsideBarBreakRaw : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw) ? math.abs(high[2] - low[2]) : (isLong ? bullishRangeBreakRaw : bearishRangeBreakRaw) ? math.abs(sidewaysRangeHigh - sidewaysRangeLow) : (isLong ? bullChannel : bearChannel) ? channelWidth : genericSwingRange
        float fallbackDistance = math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.5, syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
        float baseContextStop = isLong ? nz(lastSwingLow, close - fallbackDistance) : nz(lastSwingHigh, close + fallbackDistance)
        float contextStop = earlyNow ? (isLong ? low : high) : retestNow ? (selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? (isLong ? bullishBreakerLow : bearishBreakerHigh) : (isLong ? low : high)) : baseContextStop
        bool protectedStop = earlyNow or retestNow
        float specificStop = isLong ? bullPattern ? ihsRightShoulder : bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? low[2] : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeLow : bullChannel ? math.min(low, channelUpperNow) : bullHorizontal ? math.min(low, bullHorizontalBreakLevel) : bullTrend ? math.min(low, watchedBreakBoundaryNow) : accumulationSosEvent ? math.min(low, wyckoffRangeResistance) : bullStructure ? math.min(low, lastSwingHigh) : na : bearPattern ? hsRightShoulder : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? high[2] : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? sidewaysRangeHigh : bearChannel ? math.max(high, channelLowerNow) : bearHorizontal ? math.max(high, bearHorizontalBreakLevel) : bearTrend ? math.max(high, watchedBreakBoundaryNow) : distributionSowEvent ? math.max(high, wyckoffRangeSupport) : bearStructure ? math.max(high, lastSwingLow) : na
        float stopAnchor = protectedStop ? contextStop : not na(specificStop) ? specificStop : contextStop
        int stopKind = protectedStop ? 2 : not na(specificStop) ? 1 : 0
        int packedStopKind = stopKind * 10 + selectedEventSubKind
        string retestSource = isLong ? selectedEventSubKind == 1 ? "استمرار صاعد بعد تراجع" : selectedEventSubKind == 2 ? "اختراق ترند هابط وإعادة اختباره" : selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? "بريكر صاعد" : selectedEventSubKind == 4 ? "مقاومة أصبحت دعم" : selectedEventSubKind == 5 ? "اختبار منطقة طلب" : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? "اختبار وايكوف/سيولة صاعد" : selectedEventSubKind == 7 ? "إعادة اختبار كسر صاعد" : "كسر وهمي صاعد" : selectedEventSubKind == 1 ? "استمرار هابط بعد ارتداد" : selectedEventSubKind == 2 ? "كسر ترند صاعد وإعادة اختباره" : selectedEventSubKind == 3 ? "بريكر هابط" : selectedEventSubKind == 4 ? "دعم أصبح مقاومة" : selectedEventSubKind == 5 ? "اختبار منطقة عرض" : selectedEventSubKind == 6 ? "اختبار وايكوف/سيولة هابط" : selectedEventSubKind == 7 ? "إعادة اختبار كسر هابط" : "اختراق وهمي هابط"
        string directSuffix = isLong ? bullChannel ? " | اختراق قناة" : bullHorizontal ? " | اختراق مقاومة" : bullTrend ? " | اختراق خط اتجاه" : "" : bearChannel ? " | كسر قناة" : bearHorizontal ? " | كسر دعم" : bearTrend ? " | كسر خط اتجاه" : ""
        string directSource = isLong ? bullishInsideBarBreakRaw ? "انسايد بار" : bullishRangeBreakRaw ? "اختراق نموذج" : accumulationSosEvent ? "قوة (SOS)" : bullishChoch ? "تغير صاعد (CHoCH)" : "كسر صاعد (BOS)" : bearishInsideBarBreakRaw ? "انسايد بار" : bearishRangeBreakRaw ? "كسر نموذج" : distributionSowEvent ? "ضعف (SOW)" : bearishChoch ? "تغير هابط (CHoCH)" : "كسر هابط (BOS)"
        string earlySource = isLong ? (bullishSweep ? "انعكاس مبكر | سيولة" : bullishImportantCandleConfirmation ? "انعكاس مبكر | شمعة" : bullishRsiDivergence ? "انعكاس مبكر | دايفرجنس" : highVolumeBottomZoneEvent ? "انعكاس مبكر | حجم" : "انعكاس مبكر") : (bearishSweep ? "انعكاس مبكر | سيولة" : bearishImportantCandleConfirmation ? "انعكاس مبكر | شمعة" : bearishRsiDivergence ? "انعكاس مبكر | دايفرجنس" : highVolumePeakZoneEvent ? "انعكاس مبكر | حجم" : "انعكاس مبكر")
        string source = earlyNow ? earlySource : retestNow ? retestSource : isLong and bullPattern ? "رأس وكتفين مقلوب" : not isLong and bearPattern ? "رأس وكتفين" : directSource + directSuffix
        int sourceKind = earlyNow ? ((isLong ? bullishSweep : bearishSweep) ? SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION : (isLong ? bullishImportantCandleConfirmation : bearishImportantCandleConfirmation) ? SETUP_SOURCE_CANDLE : (isLong ? bullishRsiDivergence : bearishRsiDivergence) ? SETUP_SOURCE_DIVERGENCE : SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER) : retestNow ? (selectedEventSubKind == 6 or selectedEventSubKind == 8 ? SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION : SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER) : bullPattern or bearPattern or bullishInsideBarBreakRaw or bearishInsideBarBreakRaw or bullishRangeBreakRaw or bearishRangeBreakRaw ? SETUP_SOURCE_PATTERN : accumulationSosEvent or distributionSowEvent ? SETUP_SOURCE_AUCTION : SETUP_SOURCE_OTHER
        bool sameSetup = candidate.dir == eventDirection and candidate.eventKind == selectedEventKind and candidate.stopKind % 10 == selectedEventSubKind and not na(candidate.ref) and not na(reference) and math.abs(candidate.ref - reference) <= math.max(activeRetestTolerance, ctx.retestToleranceValue)
        bool preserveQualified = sameSetup and candidate.qualified and candidate.priority > selectedEventPriority and needsParticipation and not qualifiedNow
        // لا تسمح لإشارة أدنى أولوية بمحو إعداد مؤهل ما زال صالحا.
        bool preserveStrongerQualifiedCandidate = candidate.dir == eventDirection and not sameSetup and candidate.qualified and candidate.priority > selectedEventPriority
        if (not preserveQualified or not needsParticipation or qualifiedNow) and not preserveStrongerQualifiedCandidate
            bool usesGuard = isLong ? bullHorizontal or bullTrend or bullStructure or retestNow and not na(reference) : bearHorizontal or bearTrend or bearStructure or retestNow and not na(reference)
            float guardLevel = isLong ? bullHorizontal ? bullHorizontalBreakLevel : bullTrend ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : bullStructure ? lastSwingHigh : retestNow ? reference : na : bearHorizontal ? bearHorizontalBreakLevel : bearTrend ? watchedBreakBoundaryNow : bearStructure ? lastSwingLow : retestNow ? reference : na
            f_set_setup_candidate(candidate, eventDirection, selectedEventKind, selectedEventPriority, reference, measuredRange, stopAnchor, packedStopKind, usesGuard, guardLevel, sourceKind, source, directNow, needsParticipation, needsFvg, (sameSetup and candidate.qualified) or qualifiedNow, (sameSetup and candidate.transitionEligible) or transitionNow, (sameSetup and candidate.reversalEligible) or reversalNow, ctx.retestToleranceValue)
            activeRetestTolerance := nz(candidate.tolerance, ctx.retestToleranceValue)
    int activeMaximumBars = candidate.directBreak ? math.max(ctx.retestWindowValue, maximumBars) : maximumBars
    bool withinWindow = candidate.dir != 0 and not na(candidate.eventBar) and bar_index - candidate.eventBar <= activeMaximumBars
    bool stopHolds = candidate.dir == 1 ? na(candidate.stop) or close >= candidate.stop - ctx.breakBufferValue : candidate.dir == -1 ? na(candidate.stop) or close <= candidate.stop + ctx.breakBufferValue : false
    bool breakHolds = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 1.5 : close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 1.5)
    bool fvgFilterPass = not candidate.needsFvg or (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.fvgBullPassValue : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.fvgBearPassValue : false)
    bool followThroughPass = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance * 0.25 : candidate.dir == -1 ? close <= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance * 0.25 : false)
    if barstate.isconfirmed and breakHolds and followThroughPass and (candidate.directBreak ? (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.bullDirectQuality : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.bearDirectQuality : false) : (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.bullCandleQuality : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.bearCandleQuality : false)) and (not candidate.needsQualifiers or ctx.volumePassValue) and fvgFilterPass
        candidate.qualified := true
    bool entryQualified = not candidate.needsQualifiers or candidate.qualified
    bool active = withinWindow and stopHolds and breakHolds
    float entryReferenceRange = math.max(nz(candidate.rng, ctx.safeAtrValue * 2.0), syminfo.mintick * 10.0)
    float directMaximumExtension = ctx.adaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.25, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.60, entryReferenceRange * 0.25)) : math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.75, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.85, entryReferenceRange * 0.30))
    float retestMaximumDistance = ctx.adaptiveEntryQuality ? math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.35, entryReferenceRange * 0.18)) : math.min(ctx.safeAtrValue * 1.25, math.max(ctx.safeAtrValue * 0.50, entryReferenceRange * 0.20))
    bool minimumBreakLevelPass = not candidate.directBreak or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.dir == 1 ? close >= candidate.ref - activeRetestTolerance : close <= candidate.ref + activeRetestTolerance)
    bool exceptionalMomentumQuality = ctx.earlyEngineEnabled and candidate.directBreak and candidate.qualified and ctx.volumePassValue and ctx.bodyRatioValue >= 0.55 and (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.closeLocationValue >= 0.72 : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.closeLocationValue <= 0.28 : false)
    bool regularExtensionPass = not ctx.preventExtended or na(candidate.ref) or (candidate.directBreak ? (candidate.dir == 1 ? close <= candidate.ref + directMaximumExtension : close >= candidate.ref - directMaximumExtension) : math.abs(close - candidate.ref) <= retestMaximumDistance)
    float momentumExtensionFactor = candidate.reversalEligible ? 2.25 : 1.60
    bool momentumExtensionPass = not regularExtensionPass and exceptionalMomentumQuality and not na(candidate.ref) and (candidate.dir == 1 ? close <= candidate.ref + directMaximumExtension * momentumExtensionFactor : close >= candidate.ref - directMaximumExtension * momentumExtensionFactor)
    candidate.momentumExtensionUsed := candidate.directBreak and momentumExtensionPass
    bool extensionPass = regularExtensionPass or momentumExtensionPass
    bool entryLevelPass = minimumBreakLevelPass and extensionPass
    bool directQualityPass = not candidate.directBreak or (candidate.dir == 1 ? ctx.bullDirectQuality : candidate.dir == -1 ? ctx.bearDirectQuality : false)
    int localGateCode = not entryQualified ? 1 : not entryLevelPass ? 2 : not directQualityPass ? 3 : 0
    [activeMaximumBars, active, (marketChartAllowed and entrySessionPass and active and entryQualified and entryLevelPass and directQualityPass and candidate.dir == 1 and vwapLongPass), (marketChartAllowed and entrySessionPass and active and entryQualified and entryLevelPass and directQualityPass and candidate.dir == -1 and vwapShortPass), localGateCode]
SetupInputContext setupInputContext = SetupInputContext.new(firstAnalysisSessionBar, chartSeconds, safeAtr, ema20, movingAverageBias, resolvedStructureBias, higherWeightedScore, vsaSignal, closeLocation, bodyRatio, retestWindow, retestTolerance, breakBuffer, enableEarlyOpportunityEngine, requireHigherTimeframeAlignment, useVolumeFilter, useFvgFilter, useAdaptiveEntryQuality, preventExtendedEntries, volumePass, fvgBullPass, fvgBearPass, bullishDirectBreakQuality, bearishDirectBreakQuality, bullishBreakCandleQuality, bearishBreakCandleQuality, entryMode)
[_, longCandidateActive, longActionableRaw, _, _] = f_update_setup_candidate_state(longCandidateState, 1, setupInputContext)
[_, shortCandidateActive, _, shortActionableRaw, _] = f_update_setup_candidate_state(shortCandidateState, -1, setupInputContext)
bool actionableLongSetup = longActionableRaw
bool actionableShortSetup = shortActionableRaw
// ICT premium/discount is tied to the ordered, confirmed swing leg. It is a
// location preference for retests only, never an independent breakout trigger.
[dealingRangeEvidenceReady, dealingRangeSignal, _] = f_dealing_range_context(usePremiumDiscountContext, (longCandidateActive and not longCandidateState.directBreak) or (shortCandidateActive and not shortCandidateState.directBreak), swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar, close)
[_, _, _, fibRetracement236, fibRetracement382, fibRetracement500, fibRetracement618, fibRetracement786, fibRetracement1000] = f_fib_retracement_context(useFibRetracement, swingLevelsCompatible, lastSwingHigh, lastSwingLow, lastSwingHighBar, lastSwingLowBar)

var array<label> rightLineLabels = array.new<label>(16, na)
if barstate.islast
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 0, marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not na(cleanResistanceLevel), cleanResistanceLevel, "أقرب مقاومة", color.white, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 1, marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not na(cleanSupportLevel), cleanSupportLevel, "أقرب دعم", #F59E0B, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 2, marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not na(cleanResistanceLevel2), cleanResistanceLevel2, "المقاومة التالية", color.new(color.white, 15), safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 3, marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not na(cleanSupportLevel2), cleanSupportLevel2, "الدعم التالي", color.new(#F59E0B, 15), safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 4, marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not cleanChartMode and not na(priorHigherTimeframeResistance), priorHigherTimeframeResistance, "مقاومة HTF", bearColor, safeAtr)
    f_sync_right_line_label(rightLineLabels, 5, marketChartAllowed and showHorizontalSr and not cleanChartMode and not na(priorHigherTimeframeSupport), priorHigherTimeframeSupport, "دعم HTF", bullColor, safeAtr)

float fibStopMinimumDistance = math.max(safeAtr * 0.35, syminfo.mintick * 2.0)
